# The board's days, read whole (task 026b)

Made by `node docs/strategy/board/tools/whole.mjs` from the board watch's reads (`logs/board/`), offline, no request: 15 full reads from 2026-11-24/0917Z to 2026-11-25/1600Z, 30.7 h, 696 account-intervals between consecutive reads. Left out: 2026-11-24/1400Z (partial) and 2026-11-24/0921Z (four minutes after the read before it). An account is in an interval when it is in both reads (the 50 read are the ladder's first 50 and ten either side of alverus, so a few come and go). Counters are the account's (achievements are per account); a counter marked ⊤ reached its total in the interval, so its move is cut. Skills: cb combat, mi mining, wo woodcutting, fi fishing, wc weaponcrafting, gc gearcrafting, jc jewelrycrafting, co cooking, al alchemy. ⚡ an event's tile.

XP/h while training: over the intervals in which the craft gained XP. "for no XP": the monster is more than 10 levels under the character at that read (no XP by the rule of report 012); "stands at": on the tile, no combat XP in the interval.

**The lines this fleet can act on.**
- **A craft is levelled in bursts, from the bank.** 19 crafters of 17 accounts gained 30,000 XP or more in a window of 24 h or
  less; while their craft moved they made a median **7,032 XP an hour** (2,359 to 77,288: uaprsf's frtCraft 154,578 XP in one
  two-hour interval, Wudagem's Zeni 150,670 in another, shuxart's Kellhus 64,874), in a median 8.7 h of crafting. Second made
  **1,745** and Third **1,071**, each hunting its own drops between crafts (Second at wolves and highwaymen, Third at mushmush
  and wolves). From weaponcrafting 23 to 30 is 102,400 XP: 59 h at Second's pace, 15 h at the median's.
- **The recipe is the level-30 tier from 30 to 40**, up to 10 levels under the craft (it still gives XP): gold_mask,
  royal_skeleton_armor and _pants, gold_ring, emerald_ring, gold_boots, royal_skeleton_ring, gold_helm; elderwood_staff and
  gold_pickaxe for weapons. Below 30 the board shows two, both a level-15 ring 6–10 levels under the craft from the bank: Elaus_'s
  Broey air_ring at 25–28 (its window 12,590 XP an hour), Everywon1 water_ring at 21–25 (19,172).
- **Gold is the shared material.** In 14 of the 19 windows a character of the account stood at gold_rocks, gathered gold ore or
  made gold bars (Turbofish's Jade 24 h, automode's pilot 24 h, krolov's Darina 18 h, Elemental's George and Fredrick, shuxart's
  Achamian and Mimara; Zetark's Flintvein: the account's gold_ore +2,156 and gold_bar +47 in 30 h). Every level-30 recipe above
  asks 7–8 gold bars.
- **The drops come from fighters who get no XP for them**: Turbofish's Orchid (46) at the vampire (24), krolov's Tarina at the
  vampire and the demon, automode's cruise at owlbear and vampire, Trobyss at cyclops and death knight, brinko's Capablanca at
  owlbear, vampire and ogre.
- **The crafter is usually a fighter too, and its training is the stock crafted at once.** 14 of the 19 crafters stood within 5
  levels of their account's top combat (the board's fleets are mostly even); the other five are workers (krolov's Karina, combat
  17; Steavi's Struff, 30 of 39). Between bursts a crafter fights, gathers or farms drops like the others.
- So, for this fleet: a gold miner and gold bars in the bank; First and Fifth bank the drops of the vampire, imp, owlbear, death
  knight, cyclops and demon as they pass; the crafters craft batches the bank feeds — a level-15 to level-20 recipe until 30, the
  level-30 tier from 30.

**What the data cannot show.** The bank: a burst of 30,000–77,000 XP in two hours is crafted from stock no read sees, and how
long it took to gather is unknown. The products: crafting counters exist only for bars, planks, cooked fish, potions and four
single gear pieces, so a gear recipe is read from the bags (the product, or the share of its ingredients, at a read) — "?" where
the bag held neither, and another recipe of the same level from the same gold bars may be the true one. The recycling counter
counts recycles, not crafts (this fleet banks some products; uaprsf's frtCraft: 85 recycles for 154,578 XP), and stops at 1,000
and 2,500. A tile is a moment: a character seen at gold_rocks at two reads may have done something else between.

**The lines this fleet can act on.**
- **First and Fifth fight the right monster for their level.** At 25–27 the vampire is the best monster fought (interval medians
  2,285–3,238 XP an hour), and their vampire intervals (3,068–3,408 at 25, 1,851–2,652 at 27) are among the best of their
  levels (the best at 27).
- **From 30 the best quarter wears a level-30 weapon** (gold_sword, perfect_bow, death_knight_sword, elderwood_staff,
  obsidian_battleaxe) and fights the vampire (median 2,939 at 30, best 4,534) and the imp (best 6,237–6,517 at 30–32; fought at
  every level from 28 to 44, the most fought at 32, 33, 37 and 38). Owlbear is the best at 34–35 and 39 (6,818–8,425),
  cultist_acolyte at 42–43, goblin_wolfrider and bat at 44–46 (7,700–9,500). The battlestaff leads the best quarter's weapons up
  to 27; from 29 they are level 25–40 but for a steel cleaver (20) at 39 and gathering tools.
- **Seconds a kill for the planning chat** (B.3, upper bounds): the imp at 31–33 with a level-25 skull wand, eating cooked bass:
  95 s a kill, 100 XP (rs_appez's rs_alice and rs_eve, four intervals; cooked rat meat and bass in the bag); at 37–38 with a death knight sword and apples: 200–206 s
  (chewbaccoin's Vador); the cultist acolyte at 38 with an elderwood staff: 116 s (Elaus_'s Salty).

**What the data cannot show.** What a character fought between two reads: a tile is a moment, and 237 of the 1,020 stints have
no monster tile at either read and no single kill counter ("?"). Kill counters exist for some monsters only and most stop early
(the vampire's at 250, the spider's at 1,000), so B.3 isolates only 9 intervals. Wins, losses, potions drunk and food eaten
between reads; group fights.

**The lines this fleet can act on.**
- **The 28–32 band's best quarter** (64 intervals, 30 characters) wears a level-30 weapon (gold_sword 19, perfect_bow 8,
  elderwood_staff 5), slime_shield 20 (56 of 64), piggy_helmet 25 or gold_helm 30, steel_armor 20 or royal_skeleton_armor 30,
  royal_skeleton_pants 30 or steel_legs_armor 20, old_boots or steel_boots 20, two rings of the adept, a dreadful (20) or topaz
  (25) amulet, the healing rune, novice guide, lost world map and altar doll — and no potion: 122 of 128 utility slots empty.
- **First and Fifth match it on the rune, the rings and the artifacts** and fall short on the weapon (battlestaff 20 against 30),
  the shield (iron 10 against slime 20), First's body (adventurer_vest 10 against steel 20 or royal skeleton 30) and the amulet
  (wisdom 15 against dreadful 20 or topaz 25).
- **What the crafts make now** (wc 23, gc 25, jc 23): steel_armor, steel_boots, steel_legs_armor (gc 20; spider leg, ogre skin,
  skeleton skull, a king slimeball), dreadful_amulet and ring_of_chance (jc 20; a king slimeball each — the king slime is a boss,
  Lv 15), slime_shield (gc 20, king slimeballs), topaz_amulet (jc 25, wolf hair) at jewelry 25. The weapon and the gold set wait
  for 30; gold_sword, perfect_bow, gold_helm and gold_shield ask demon horns — the demon event only — and the piggy pieces a
  full moon vampire cape, another event.
- **The level-30 pieces with no event or boss drop** (the catalog, every leaf's sources): greater_dreadful_staff (wc 30: cyclops
  eye, vampire blood, ogre eye, red cloth — worn by the 38–42 band's best quarter 13 times), royal_skeleton_helmet, _armor and
  _pants, flying_boots (gc 30), gold_ring, emerald_ring, royal_skeleton_ring, lost_amulet, prospecting_amulet (jc 30); at 25
  dreadful_staff and skull_wand (wc 25), piggy_pants, snakeskin_legs_armor (gc 25), topaz and sapphire amulets (jc 25). Every
  other level-30 weapon of the board (gold_sword, perfect_bow: demon horns; elderwood_staff, obsidian_battleaxe: lizard skin of the
  bandit lizard event) waits for an event, or the death knight's sword (1 in 600).

**What the data cannot show.** The bank (what a fleet owns but does not wear), why a set is worn (Zetark changed 135 slots in
30 h, this fleet 174: both swap per job), and whether a piece was crafted, dropped, bought on the market or traded.

**The lines this fleet can act on.**
- **After 39 the commonest are gathering and crafting milestones and four combat ones.** Of the 31 accounts above 39:
  expert_miner (17, done here), expert_lumberjack (13; dead_wood 3,723/6,000), worker (done), professional_fisherman and
  expert_fisherman (salmon and bass 0/6,000), cabinet_maker (dead_wood_plank 5/300), professional_miner (mithril 0/6,000),
  diamond_in_the_sky (3 points; two diamonds); cultist_purifier (4, 11 accounts; cultist acolyte Lv 33, 0/500), lich_please (2;
  one lich), cult_destroyer (2; the cultist emperor, an event), life_lover (4; 24 life crystal shards).
- **The next cheap points here**: expert_lumberjack (2,277 dead wood left), cabinet_maker (295 dead wood planks), then the
  fishing and mining milestones of a worker; the combat ones ask a level of 33–40.
- **60 → 80** (6 accounts): king_of_the_goblins, clean_the_lab, cultist_purifier, grand_master, crafted_with_honor — goblins and
  cultists of 33–40.

**What the data cannot show.** Why an account chose its order; a counter of a completed achievement stops, so what an account did
after it is unseen; a ladder's points can lag its achievements by a read (two accounts on 11-25 16:00).

Favors and tasks: the `favor` and `task` counters (A Favored Champion 500, Tasks Coins Fan 1,000). Monsters tasks: of the character-reads holding a task. Event kills: kill counters of monsters that only events bring (bandit_lizard, corrupted_ogre, corrupted_owlbear, cultist_emperor, demon, duskworm, echoless_bat, efreet_sultan, full_moon_vampire, grimlet, red_dragon, sea_marauder, solar_desert_scorpion) — most have none. Uses: the `use` counters (foods of Carnivore and Gourmet Chef, the teleport potions of Art of Teleportation, the corrupted fruit), not every food. Left bags: food and utility potions whose count in a bag or a utility slot fell between two reads — eaten, drunk or banked. Idle: a character-read whose `cooldown_expiration` lies more than ten minutes before the read.

**The lines this fleet can act on.**
- **Tasks: this fleet finished none in 30.7 h** (the task counter stood at 403) where the median account finished 8.6 a day and
  the best quarter 23.4. First and Fifth hold a chicken (0/248) and a flying snake (0/367) task while they fight vampires; the
  fleets that level fastest fight what their task names (B; Turbofish's Orchid at the vampire with a vampire task).
- **Favors**: 7.8 a day, the median (best quarter 10.9). **Event kills**: 9.4 a day (12 demons), the best quarter 15.2.
- **Food**: the median account's bags lost 297 cooked food a day (best quarter 584); this fleet's 73. **Idle**: 0% of this
  fleet's character-reads, as the median.

**What the data cannot show.** Whether food or a potion that left a bag was eaten or banked (this fleet's 739 potions a day are
its train loop's deposits and withdrawals); `use` counters cover a few foods and the teleport potions only, and stop at their
totals (⊤); a task cancelled is not counted.

**The three twins** by combat levels gained since 11-24 09:17 among the accounts at top combat 28–36 then: Zetark (+9),
rs_appez (+7), ArmaRGool (+7). This fleet gained **+10** (First and Fifth 24 → 28) — more than any of them.

**Ten lines: what they did that alverus did not.**
1. Zetark's crafters fight at their craft's level: Ashmere (combat 32, weaponcrafting 30) and Sunbloom (combat 30, gearcrafting
   30, jewelry 25, alchemy 43) made the level-30 weapons and armour its fighters wear; this fleet's crafts stand at 23 / 25 / 23.
2. Zetark's Flintvein mined gold (9 of 15 reads at gold_rocks, 11-24 12:00 to 11-25 06:00): the account's gold ore +2,156 and gold bars +47 — the
   level-30 tier's gold; this fleet's Fourth mined gold for a milestone and stood at the bank for 10 reads.
3. Zetark wore a level-30 weapon at 30 (Ashmere: gold_sword, then perfect_bow, from 11-24 22:00); Zetark himself perfect_bow,
   obsidian_battleaxe and elderwood_staff by fight.
4. Zetark's three fighters fought the imp (28) at 28–38 (Ashmere 3,360–3,895 XP an hour at 30–31) — the imp drops the imp tail
   and the obsidian of the level-30 weapons.
5. Ashmere wore royal skeleton and gold pieces (30) at 30, which only Sunbloom's gearcrafting 30 could make; First wears a
   level-10 vest at 28.
6. rs_appez kept two fishers at the bass spot all day (fishing 3,500 an hour, cooking 41–47) and its fighters carried cooked bass
   (rs_eve 33–103): rs_eve made 3,750–5,929 XP an hour at the imp at 33–34 with a level-25 wand.
7. rs_appez finished 43 tasks in 30 h (and imp kills +432); this fleet none.
8. rs_appez and ArmaRGool levelled no craft (25, and 21–24): their fighters stand at 32–36 and 29 — the two twins that left the
   crafts gained fewer levels than Zetark.
9. ArmaRGool: all five at the pig and the trout spot, idle for 8 h (00:00–08:00 on 11-25) — nothing to copy.
10. Zetark and rs_appez wear the rune and the artifacts this fleet wears (rs_appez a perfect pearl beside them); the gap is the
    weapon, the armour of level 25–30, the food and the tasks.

**What the data cannot show.** The twins' banks and market trades; whether a piece was made by the account (the "who could make
it" column says who had the craft level at that read, not who made it).
